Title: Maris Amri et Slibae De patriarchis Nestorianorum commentaria (archive.org catalogue title). Pars prior: Maris textus Arabicus (file line 9265) and Maris versio Latina (file line 16247), ex codicibus Vaticanis edidit ac Latine reddidit Henricus Gismondi S.J.
Creator: Mari ibn Sulayman (twelfth century), the section on the patriarchs of the East from his Kitab al-Majdal (Book of the Tower); Enrico (Henricus) Gismondi S.J. (ed. and Latin trans.)
Publisher: Romae: C. de Luigi (archive.org catalogue); printed by the Tipografia della Casa Editrice Italiana, Roma 1899 (file lines 9241 and 9300)
Publication Date: 1899 (file line 9241; Gismondi's preface is dated Romae Idibus Februarii MDCCCXCIX, OCR 'MDCGGXCIX', at file line 9178)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1899); the item record carries no rights field
Language: lat (Latin only recovered: a character count found no Arabic script, so Mari's Arabic text is not usable from this OCR. Both parts are scanned in reverse page order, the volume having been scanned from the Arabic side: the Arabic text part occupies file lines 1 to about 9265, its title page 'Maris textus Arabicus' at 9265 and Gismondi's Latin preface to it at 9178 to 9241; the Latin version occupies about 9280 to the end, its title page 'Maris versio Latina' at 16247, and its pages run backwards, page 134 at 9485, page 95 at 11431, page 52 at 13610, page 13 at 15584.)
Source: archive.org item marisamrietslib1v2mari (PIMS - University of Toronto; catalogue volume 1-2), full text at https://archive.org/download/marisamrietslib1v2mari/marisamrietslib1v2mari_djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-10-17 by the Shared-Events source-research thread (Era 2 worker C); the byte count of the downloaded file (824,808) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected, and the reversed page order is left as scanned. Rights basis: an 1899 Rome printing is out of US copyright under this project's pd-us-by-date rule.
Content note: Mari's lives of the catholicoi of the East in Gismondi's Latin (read the Latin pages in descending file order): Tomarsa and the accession of Yazdgard at file line 15065, Isaac's death and burial at al-Mada'in after eleven years at 14915, Ahai at 14918, Yahballaha at 14932, and Yazdgard's dealings with the Christians around 14886. A twelfth-century Church of the East memory witness built from older Syriac histories; never evidence of 410 or 424 on its own. The Arabic text of Amr and Sliba (pars altera) is vendored separately.

duorum  aliorum  codicum  adhibita  collatione,  tjpis  expressum  ac  latina  inter-

aut  erratae  scriptionis  emendationes,  quaeque  ad  modum  coniecturae  succur- 
rebant  suggerendae,  sive  in  textu  edilo,  sive  in  olenclio  W.  LL.  notandas 
curavi.  Dictionis  soloecismos  retinui,  nisi  nimiae  quandoque  offensioni  Lectori 
essent,  ut  monui  in  editione  recensionum  Slibae  et  Amri.  Anachronismos 
ahosque  bistoricos  opeiMs  errores  castigandos  non  censui,  graviores  enim  sunt 
quam  qui  eruditum  lectorem  fugere  possint.  De  fontibus  autem  unde  tam 
locupletem  notitiarum  copiam  auctor  hausit,  tractationem  instituere  praema- 
turum  esse  opus  visum  est,  quandoquidem  antiquiora  scripta,  ex  quibus  ma- 
nare  illa  censeri  potest,  aha  nondum  explorata  sunt,  alia  nunc  viri  docti 
parant  in  lucem  edenda:  in  quibus  complura  sane  a  nostro  auctore  memo- 
rata  plenius  utique  exposita  reperientur,  at  non  pauca  forte  unius  eius  fidei 
adscripta  manebunt.  Idipsum  quadantenus  et  quoad  Shbae  recensionem  puta- 
verim,  non  uno  enim  Maris  opere  illum  usum  esse  qui  sedulo  utriusqiie  11- 
brum  inspicit  deprehendet.

ptis  dictionibus  atque  sententiis;  adeo  ut  a  proposito  desistendi  haud  semel 
voluntas  fuerit.  Verum  pigebat  inoditum  linquere  nobile  opus,  unde  haustam,

exemplar  mihi  transcribi  mandaveram,  opus  aggredi  me  posse  censui,  facto
